Abstract: We investigate the recent proposal of holographic volume complexity for timelike subregions \cite{Alishahiha:2025xml} in the framework of static patch holography for de Sitter spacetime. Using the stretched-horizon prescription, we compute the timelike subregion complexity as a function of the subregion duration for pure de Sitter and Schwarzschild de Sitter geometries. In pure de Sitter spacetime, the timelike subregion complexity displays exponential growth for short durations, and hyperfast growth near a maximal duration, paralleling the features of spacelike volume complexity \cite{Jorstad:2022mls}. For Schwarzschild de Sitter, when the stretched horizon is near the cosmological horizon, the behavior broadly remains similar to pure de Sitter. However, when the stretched horizon is near the black hole horizon, the hyperfast growth for long durations is replaced by nonlinear growth regime. Along the way, we also compute the corresponding timelike holographic entanglement entropy for de Sitter and Schwarzschild de Sitter.

</details>


### [103] [A-type Sigma Models from Differential Poisson Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29668)
*Cesar Arias,Per Sundell*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究微分Poisson sigma模型（DPSM）在辛情形下的经典约化，证明其定义了一类以辛流形（不必为Kähler）为目标空间的A-模型。DPSM是一个协变一阶sigma模型，其分次目标空间为Poisson流形M的奇宇称切丛T[1]M，其分次Poisson张量编码了Ω^•(M)上的微分Poisson括号，以联络Γ及其转置Γ̃协变写出。在非退化情形下，Jacobi恒等式迫使Γ平坦，而约化作用量的四次耦合由Γ̃的曲率（由Γ的挠率诱导）给出。因此DPSM选择了一类辛类，其中A-模型的曲率耦合获得了源于一阶Poisson结构的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的A-模型通常要求目标空间为Kähler流形，其曲率耦合缺乏更深层的几何起源。作者希望找到一类更广泛的辛目标空间上的A-模型，其曲率四次耦合项可以从一阶Poisson sigma模型的约化中自然涌现，从而建立A-模型与Poisson几何之间的新联系，并在链层次上揭示可观测复形上的微分Poisson结构。

Method: 作者从微分Poisson sigma模型出发，将其在辛（非退化）情形下进行经典约化。利用T[1]M上的分次Poisson张量编码Ω^•(M)上的微分Poisson括号，并引入联络Γ及其转置Γ̃进行协变表达。通过Jacobi恒等式分析约束Γ的平坦性，计算约化作用得到四次耦合项为Γ̃的曲率（由Γ的挠率诱导）。随后通过具体例子（仿射辛目标、辛环面、Kodaira–Thurston流形）和排除情形（CP^n、K3曲面）刻画此类辛类的边界，并通过T[1]M上的分次父几何赋予Ω^•(M)微分Poisson括号及Ω^•(M)[1]上严格的L_∞-代数结构。

Result: 证明了在非退化情形下DPSM经典约化给出A-模型，其四次曲率耦合起源于一阶Poisson结构；非Kähler辛目标（如Kodaira–Thurston流形）可以作为A-模型的目标空间；而CP^n和K3曲面被排除在此类之外；同时建立了链层次上的微分Poisson结构和严格L_∞-代数结构，这是通常Kähler表述中所不具备的。

Conclusion: DPSM约化提供了一类新的A-模型簇，其目标空间为满足特定条件的辛流形（不必Kähler），曲率耦合具有清晰的一阶几何起源。这种构造在链层次上赋予可观测复形自然的微分Poisson结构，为理解A-模型在非Kähler几何中的推广开辟了新途径，也揭示了连接Poisson几何、微分分级几何与拓扑弦理论的新框架。

Abstract: We study the differential Poisson sigma model (DPSM) in the symplectic case and show that its classical reduction defines a distinguished class of A-type models on symplectic targets, not necessarily Kähler. The DPSM is a covariant first-order sigma model whose graded target is the parity-shifted tangent bundle $T[1]M$ of a Poisson manifold $M$. Its graded Poisson tensor encodes a differential Poisson bracket on $C(T[1]M)\congΩ^\bullet(M)$, written covariantly in terms of a connection $Γ$ and its transpose $\widetildeΓ$. In the nondegenerate case, the Jacobi identities force $Γ$ to be flat, while the quartic coupling of the reduced action is given by the curvature of $\widetildeΓ$, induced by the torsion of $Γ$. Thus, the DPSM selects a symplectic class in which the A-model curvature coupling acquires a first-order Poisson origin. We describe this class through examples and obstructions; $\mathbb{CP}^n$ and K3 surfaces are excluded, while affine symplectic targets, symplectic tori, and the Kodaira--Thurston manifold furnish explicit examples. The graded parent geometry on $T[1]M$ equips $Ω^\bullet(M)$ with a differential Poisson bracket and $Ω^\bullet(M)[1]$ with a strict $L_\infty$-algebra structure, equipping the observable complex with a natural chain-level differential Poisson structure that is not manifest in the usual Kähler formulation of the A-model.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [A relative Euclidean thickening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29631)
*John R. Klein*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文针对有限CW对(K,L)，构造了一个Poincaré三元组(P;∂₀P,∂₁P)以及弱同伦等价(K,L)→(P,∂₀P)，且该Poincaré三元组具有平凡的Spivak法向纤维化。证明采用同伦论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手术理论（surgery theory）和流形分类问题中，Poincaré三元组是研究嵌入和配边结构的重要工具。作者旨在证明任意有限CW对都可以被一个具有特殊性质（平凡Spivak法向纤维化）的Poincaré三元组所替代，从而为后续的几何或代数拓扑应用提供同伦论基础。

Method: 作者采用纯同伦论的方法进行构造。具体而言，通过同伦论技术（如同伦极限、纤维化构造等）从给定的有限CW对(K,L)出发，构造一个满足Poincaré对偶性的三元组(P;∂₀P,∂₁P)，并建立从(K,L)到(P,∂₀P)的弱同伦等价。同时确保所构造的Poincaré三元组具有平凡的Spivak法向纤维化。

Result: 成功证明了：对于任意有限CW对(K,L)，存在一个Poincaré三元组(P;∂₀P,∂₁P)及弱同伦等价(K,L)→(P,∂₀P)，且该Poincaré三元组的Spivak法向纤维化是平凡的。

Conclusion: 该结果表明任何有限CW对在同伦意义下都可以嵌入到一个带有平凡正常纤维化的Poincaré三元组中，这为手术理论及相关领域提供了重要工具，将几何问题转化为纯同伦论问题来处理。

Abstract: For a finite CW pair $(K,L)$, we show how to construct a Poincaré triad $(P;\partial_0 P,\partial_1 P)$ and a weak homotopy equivalence $(K,L) \to (P,\partial_0 P)$. Furthermore, the Poincaré triad has a trivial Spivak normal fibration. The proof is homotopy-theoretic.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [A user's guide to PINNs in geometric analysis: lessons from the asymptotic Plateau problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28733)
*Tancredi Schettini Gherardini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文是arXiv:2605.26234v2的方法论姊妹篇，讨论了基于物理信息神经网络（PINNs）构造双曲空间中渐近于给定纽结的近似极小圆盘框架中两个关键的工程实践：一是将几何信息编码进模型架构以保证边界条件和无穷远渐近性对所有可学习参数精确成立；二是通过前向传播二阶jets替代嵌套反向模式自动微分、以及一次性编译残差计算图来大幅降低训练成本（加速约40-50倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望分享在PINNs应用于微分几何问题时决定方法成败的关键工程细节，使其方法论文能够帮助其他研究者在自己的几何问题中更有效地部署PINNs。

Method: 本文不重新回顾原论文的结果，而是聚焦于两个决定性工程方面：（1）将双曲空间的几何结构编码进神经网络架构，使边界条件和无穷远渐近性对任意参数取值都精确成立，从而将损失函数简化为单一分量；（2）对PDE残差评估进行工程优化，包括用前向传播二阶jets替换嵌套反向模式自动微分，以及预先编译计算图而非每步优化重建。

Result: 上述两项实现技术相结合，在相同硬件上将单步训练成本降低了约40至50倍。

Conclusion: 作者希望这些方法论讨论对希望在各自问题中部署PINNs的微分几何与几何分析领域研究者有所助益。

Abstract: This proceedings contribution elaborates on the findings of arXiv:2605.26234v2: a joint work with Marco Usula, where we introduced a machine learning framework based on physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), aimed at constructing near-minimal discs in hyperbolic space asymptotic to a prescribed knot at infinity. We used this method to provide numerical evidence for a conjecture of Joel Fine relating minimal surfaces in $H^{4}$ to the coefficients of the HOMFLY polynomial. This is a methodological companion to that paper, based on a presentation given at the 2026 edition of the workshop "DANGER: Data, Numbers, and Geometry". Rather than reviewing the results, which are presented extensively in the preprint above, we discuss the two aspects of the framework which, in our experience, determined whether the method worked at all. First, the geometry of the problem must be encoded in the architecture of the model, so that the boundary condition and asymptotics at infinity hold exactly for every value of the learnable parameters - leaving us with a single-component loss function; second, the evaluation of the PDE residual must be engineered with care to ensure that complete trainings can be performed in a reasonable time. On the latter point, we describe two implementation techniques which are not spelled out in detail in the original paper: replacing nested reverse-mode automatic differentiation with the forward propagation of second-order jets, and compiling the computational graph of the residual once instead of rebuilding it at every optimisation step. Together, on identical hardware, these two changes reduce the cost of a training step by a factor of roughly forty to fifty. We hope these methodological discussions can be useful for researchers in differential geometry and geometric analysis who wish to deploy PINNs on problems of their own.

</details>


### [106] [On the construction of geographical maps: Lagrange, Chebyshev, Darboux and Milnor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29263)
*Hideki Miyachi,Ken'Ichi Ohshika,Athanase Papadopoulos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Lagrange(1779)、Chebyshev(1856)、Darboux(1911)三篇同名文章《论地图的构造》及Milnor(1969)的论文，给出Darboux与Milnor定理的完整证明，指出这些工作的核心结论：最优地理地图的特征是其共形因子在区域边界上为常数。Milnor定理的陈述与证明被推广到更一般的设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理并连接历史上Lagrange、Chebyshev、Darboux与Milnor关于最优地图构造的经典工作，澄清Chebyshev未明确表述的"最优"含义，并为Darboux与Milnor的定理提供完整证明。

Method: 综述四篇历史论文的主要思想与相互联系，完整证明Darboux与Milnor的定理，将Milnor的定理推广到更一般的设定下并进行陈述与证明。

Result: 阐明了Chebyshev隐含的最优地图判据：共形因子在区域边界上为常数，该结论与Darboux和Milnor的结论一致；给出了两者的完整证明，并扩展了Milnor定理的适用范围。

Conclusion: 最优地理地图的特征是共形因子在边界上恒定，此结论贯穿Chebyshev、Darboux与Milnor的研究；本文通过完整证明和推广，使这些经典结果在现代框架下得到统一与完善。

Abstract: Lagrange, Chebyshev, and Darboux, in 1779, 1856, and 1911, respectively, wrote articles all bearing the same title, \emph{On the Construction of Geographical Maps}. In 1969, Milnor wrote a paper in which he refers to Chebyshev's paper, of which he provides a new formulation and proof. In the present article, we review the results of all these papers, explaining the main ideas they contain and pointing out connections between them. We give complete proofs of the statements by Darboux and Milnor, both of which aim to make explicit and provide a proof of Chebyshev's result, but whose contents are different. Although Chebyshev did not state explicitly what the word ``best'' means, his conclusion, like that of Darboux and of Milnor, is that a best geographical map is characterised by the fact that its conformal factor is constant on the boundary of the region represented. Our statement and proof of Milnor's theorem work in a more general setting than the one he gives. The final version of this paper will appear in the Handbook of Mathematics in the Arts and Sciences (second edition), ed. Bharath Sriraman, Springer, 2027.

</details>


### [107] [Branching of regular abnormal geodesics in sub-Riemannian manifolds of rank two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29315)
*Luca Rizzi,Mario Sigalotti,Nicolò Tedesco*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了秩为二的次黎曼流形中正则异常测地线的分支现象，并提供了两个新现象的实例：严格异常分支测地线的连续族，以及具有可数多个分支点累积的测地线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在次黎曼几何中，异常测地线的分支结构是一个重要的研究课题。此前对这类现象的理解尚不完整，作者希望探索秩为二的次黎曼流形中正则异常测地线是否会产生分支，并发现新的分支行为类型。

Method: 作者从理论上研究秩为二的次黎曼流形中正则异常测地线的分支性质，并通过构造具体实例来展示所发现的两个新现象：连续族的严格异常分支测地线，以及具有可数个分支点累积的测地线。

Result: 证明了秩为二的次黎曼流形中正则异常测地线可以发生分支，并给出了两个此前未知的新现象的具体例子：一是严格异常分支测地线可以形成连续族；二是存在测地线其分支点可数多地累积。

Conclusion: 这些发现丰富了次黎曼几何中异常测地线分支理论，揭示了比以往所知更为复杂的分支结构，为后续研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We study branching of regular abnormal geodesics in sub-Riemannian manifolds of rank two. As an application, we provide examples of two new phenomena: continuous families of strictly abnormal branching geodesics, and geodesics with an accumulation of countably many branching points.

</details>


### [108] [Elastic Curves via Geometric Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29654)
*Oliver Gross,Rohit Jammula,Albert Chern*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文从几何力学的视角为弹性曲线提出了一种新的等周变分刻画：曲线是弹性的当且仅当它在固定面积和体积向量的约束下是长度泛函的临界点。这些约束在刚体运动下自然变换，对应于对称性的动量变量，从而引导出一个新的离散理论，该理论无需辅助离散化曲率或材料标架，并保留Marsden-Weinstein（预）辛结构，为离散空间曲线上的哈密顿动力学（切向流、涡丝流、修正KdV流）提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性曲线是经典力学、几何与计算机图形学中的基本对象，传统上以弯曲能量在长度和挠率约束下的驻点来描述。作者希望从几何力学的角度提供一种新的等价刻画——基于等周变分（固定面积和体积向量下的长度临界点），并以此构建一种结构保持、无需辅助离散化的离散弹性曲线理论，从而为离散曲线上的哈密顿动力学提供统一的辛几何框架。

Method: 作者利用一个鲜为人知的等周变分特征：曲线为弹性曲线当且仅当它是固定面积向量与体积向量约束下长度泛函的临界点。他们证明这些约束在保取向刚体运动下自然变换，并将其识别为对称性的动量变量。随后，作者将长度、面积、体积向量等低阶积分量自然地定义到多边形曲线上，保持变换律严格满足，从而引入离散弹性曲线的变分定义。最后，他们将Marsden-Weinstein（预）辛结构推广至多边形曲线，支持离散哈密顿动力学。

Result: 作者得到了一种新的、完全变分的离散弹性曲线定义，该定义结构保持、无需离散化曲率或材料标架。该结构将Marsden-Weinstein形式自然地携带到多边形曲线上，产生了离散空间曲线上的新哈密顿动力学方法，包括离散切向流、涡丝（vortex-filament）流以及修正Korteweg-de Vries流。

Conclusion: 该工作通过等周变分刻画将连续弹性曲线的几何力学结构完整地提升到离散（多边形）情形，既保持了变换对称性与（预）辛结构，又避免了传统离散方法中引入曲率和材料标架的繁琐近似，为离散可积系统与几何数值方法提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Elastic curves are the mathematical shapes of thin elastic rods in equilibrium, with deep connections to mechanics, geometry, and computer graphics. Traditionally described as stationary points of bending energy under length and torsion constraints, their rich theory admits many equivalent characterizations. We develop a new one from the viewpoint of geometric mechanics. Our main contribution relies on a lesser-known isoperimetric characterization: a curve is elastic if and only if it is a critical point of the length functional under fixed area and volume vectors. We show that these constraints transform naturally under orientation-preserving rigid body motions, identifying them as momentum variables for these symmetries. This structure suggests a new discrete theory. We show that the low-order integral quantities length, area, and volume vectors are all naturally defined for polygonal curves, leaving the same transformation laws exactly satisfied. The resulting definition of discrete elastic curves in terms of the isoperimetric characterization restricted to discrete polygonal curves is variational, structure-preserving, and requires no auxiliary discretizations of curvature or material frames. Finally, the same structure carries the Marsden--Weinstein form, a canonical (pre-)symplectic structure on the space of curves, to polygonal curves. This yields novel approaches to Hamiltonian dynamics on discrete space curves, including tangent, vortex-filament, and modified Korteweg--de Vries flows.

</details>


### [109] [The Penrose inequality with charge for 2-convex initial data sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29447)
*Tuan Dolmen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在Dong最近引入的2-凸性条件下证明了带电荷的Penrose不等式。具体而言，对于满足带电优势能量条件和2-凸性条件的完整连通的渐近平坦爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦初始数据集，若其最外侧过去视界Σ满足|Σ|≥4πq²，则ADM质量m满足m≥√(|Σ|/16π)+q²√(π/|Σ|)，等号成立当且仅当k≡0且(M,g;E,B)等距于亚极端Reissner-Nordström时空的典型切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Penrose不等式推广到带电情形是数学广义相对论中的重要问题。Dong已在2-凸性条件下解决了无电荷情形，本文的目标是利用同样的技术框架将其推广到爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中的带电荷情形，从而建立更一般的质量-视界-电荷之间的几何约束关系。

Method: 本文建立在Dong关于无电荷情形的证明基础上，利用其P-逆平均曲率流及其弱形式化。该流仅依赖于(g,P)（P为修正的第二基本形式），因此无需修改即可适用于带电情形。本文的核心创新在于修改单调性公式以纳入额外的电荷项。对于时间对称数据(k≡0)，该流退化为经典逆平均曲率流，单调性公式退化为Jang关于带电Hawking质量的单调性。

Result: 在2-凸性条件下证明了带电Penrose不等式：m≥√(|Σ|/16π)+q²√(π/|Σ|)，并刻画了等号成立的情形——即k≡0且数据等距于亚极端Reissner-Nordström时空的典型切片。同时恢复了经典的带电黎曼Penrose不等式作为时间对称情形的特例。

Conclusion: 该结果将Dong的2-凸性框架成功推广至带电情形，弥补了带电Penrose不等式的证明空白。等号情形的刻画确保了不等式的尖锐性，而恢复的带电黎曼Penrose不等式验证了方法的自然性。

Abstract: We prove the Penrose inequality with charge under the 2-convexity condition recently introduced by Dong. More precisely, given a complete, connected and asymptotically flat Einstein-Maxwell initial data set $(M,g,k; E,B)$ satisfying the charged dominant energy and the 2-convexity conditions, with divergence-free electromagnetic vector fields $(E,B)$ and a connected outermost past apparent horizon $Σ$ that satisfies $|Σ| \geq 4πq^2$ - where $q$ is the total charge - we show that the following inequality for the ADM mass $m$ holds: $m\geq \sqrt{\frac{|Σ|}{16π}} + q^2 \sqrt{\fracπ{|Σ|}}$, with equality if and only if $k \equiv 0$ and $(M,g;E,B)$ is isometric to a canonical slice of sub-extremal Reissner-Nordström spacetime. Building on Dong's proof of the uncharged case, we use his $\mathbf{P}$-inverse mean curvature flow and its weak formulation, which only depends on $(g,\mathbf{P})$ and hence applies to the charged setting unchanged. The novelty of our work is the modification of the monotonicity formula to account for the additional charge term. For time-symmetric data ($k \equiv 0$), the flow reduces to the classical inverse mean curvature flow and our monotonicity formula to Jang's monotonicity of the charged Hawking mass, recovering the charged Riemannian Penrose inequality.

</details>


### [110] [A dichotomy for minimal submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29502)
*Tobias Holck Colding,William P. Minicozzi*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文综述了极小流形二元性定理的最新进展：极小子流形要么像空间填充曲线一样铺满空间，要么被限制住并满足定量的几何与函数论约束（如欧氏体积增长、密度收敛最优速率、复曲线刚性、刘维尔定理等）。核心机制是体积加倍定理，将几何限制转化为定量刚性，由此推广到所有维数与余维数，包括高余维Bernstein定理和全维最优稳定Bernstein定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小子流形在几何分析中占据核心地位，理解其在空间中的行为（是铺满空间还是被限制）一直是关键问题。此前许多刚性结论需要事先假设体积增长等条件，而本文综述的系列结果表明这些限制可由几何本身迫使产生，无需预先假定。此外，将三维中的经典理论（嵌入极小球面结构理论）推广到所有维数是一个重要目标。

Method: 核心方法是体积加倍定理：被限制在薄板内的平稳积分varifold在给定尺度下不能将体积增大超过一个普适因子。通过高度-超出（height-excess）界将该原理具体化，将几何限制转化为定量刚性，进而推导出欧氏体积增长、最优密度速率、复曲线刚性、刘维尔定理、Bernstein定理等系列结论。整个框架源自三维嵌入极小球面的结构理论，建立在单侧曲率估计和半空间定理之上，并以弱类比形式推广到所有维数。

Result: 作者展示：该原理在每一维数与余维数下产生次线性高度增长子流形的欧氏体积增长；在板状区域中产生最优密度速率；产生复曲线刚性与刘维尔刚性；高余维Bernstein定理；单侧体积界；以及推广Moser、Bombieri-De Giorgi-Miranda、Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck和Ecker-Huisken结果的全维最优稳定Bernstein定理。

Conclusion: 极小子流形存在普适的二元性：要么空间填充，要么受限制并被定量约束。几何限制本身足以迫使体积增长等刚性，无需预先假设。这一图景从三维嵌入极小球面理论自然延伸为所有维数下的弱类比，统一了众多经典的Bernstein型与刚性结果。

Abstract: We survey a circle of recent results showing that a minimal submanifold obeys a dichotomy: either it fills up space, spreading out like a space-filling curve, or it is confined, and confinement forces quantitative restrictions. On the confined side these restrictions are both geometric and function-theoretic: Euclidean volume growth, an optimal rate of convergence of the density, complex-curve rigidity for stable surfaces in $\bf{R}^4$, and a Liouville theorem forcing slowly growing harmonic functions on minimal disks to be constant. A prototypical restriction is Euclidean volume growth, which in these results is forced by the geometry rather than assumed. The mechanism is a volume doubling theorem that converts geometric confinement into quantitative rigidity: a stationary integral varifold trapped in a thin slab at a given scale cannot double its volume by more than a universal factor. We explain how this principle, and the height-excess bounds behind it, produce Euclidean volume growth for submanifolds of sublinearly growing height in every dimension and codimension, the optimal density rate in a slab, the complex-curve and Liouville rigidity above, a higher-codimension Bernstein theorem for disks, one-sided volume bounds, and an optimal stable Bernstein theorem in all dimensions generalizing Moser, Bombieri-De Giorgi-Miranda, Caffarelli-Nirenberg-Spruck and Ecker-Huisken.
  We also explain how this entire picture emerges from the structure theory of embedded minimal disks in $\bf{R}^3$, built on the one-sided curvature estimate and reflected globally in the half-space theorem, and how it extends as a weak analogue of that theory to all dimensions.

</details>


### [111] [$p$-Kähler structures on nilmanifolds and holomorphically parallelizable manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29506)
*Ettore Lo Giudice,Asia Mainenti*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了nil流形（具有幂零复结构）和全纯可平行化solv流形上的Alessandrini-Bassanelli猜想，并由此分类了在较低p值下允许p-Kähler结构的全纯可平行化solv流形，同时提供了紧致全纯可平行化流形（具有约化万有覆盖）中(n-2)-Kähler流形的新例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alessandrini-Bassanelli猜想是复几何中关于Kähler型结构存在性的重要未解决问题，涉及p-Kähler结构在特定类型流形（如nilmanifolds和solvmanifolds）上的刻画。解决该猜想有助于深入理解非Kähler流形的几何与拓扑性质。

Method: 通过分析nil流形上幂零复结构和全纯可平行化solv流形的几何性质，运用PDE与Lie代数方法证明Alessandrini-Bassanelli猜想；在此基础上利用p-Kähler结构的分类结果，进一步对全纯可平行化solv流形进行分类，并在具有约化万有覆盖的紧致全纯可平行化流形中构造新的(n-2)-Kähler例子。

Result: 成功证明了Alessandrini-Bassanelli猜想在两类重要流形上成立；给出了允许p-Kähler结构（低p值）的全纯可平行化solv流形的完整分类；构造了新的(n-2)-Kähler流形例子。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了p-Kähler结构理论在齐次复流形上的研究，为理解非Kähler流形的复几何结构提供了新的分类结果和具体例子。

Abstract: We prove the Alessandrini-Bassanelli conjecture on nilmanifolds with nilpotent complex structures and on holomorphically parallelizable solvmanifolds. As a consequence, we classify holomorphically parallelizable solvmanifolds admitting $p$-Kähler structures, for lower values of $p$. We further provide new examples of $(n-2)$-Kähler manifolds, in the setting of compact holomorphically parallelizable manifolds with reductive universal cover.

</details>


### [112] [Regularity of branched stable minimal immersed hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29632)
*Gaoming Wang,Xuwen Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了分支稳定极小浸入超曲面非分支奇异集的Hausdorff维数尖锐界：n=2时非分支奇异集为空，n=3时为离散集，n≥4时Hausdorff维数至多为n-3。并通过构造R^4中的非平坦稳定极小锥及其与欧氏因子的乘积，证明了该正则性界在每一维n≥3中都是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解分支稳定极小超曲面的奇异集结构是几何测度论与极小曲面理论中的核心问题。已有结果对非分支奇异集的正则性估计不够精确，需要建立尖锐的维数上界，并验证其最优性。

Method: 证明的主要工具包括：(1) 广义Schoen不等式，用于控制非分支奇异集附近的曲率行为；(2) 相应的分支片层定理（branched sheeting theorem），用于处理稳定古典锥与超平面并集附近的奇异结构分析。结合这两种工具对非分支奇异集的局部结构进行精细刻画。

Result: 得到非分支奇异集维数的尖锐上界：n=2时为空集，n=3时为离散集，n≥4时Hausdorff维数不超过n-3。同时在R^4中构造了由分支极小浸入产生的非平坦稳定极小锥，其顶点为非分支奇异点；通过乘积构造得到Hausdorff维数恰好为n-3的例子，证明了界的最优性。

Conclusion: 分支稳定极小浸入超曲面的非分支奇异集具有比一般奇异集更严格的正则性，且所得维数上界在所有维度中都是最优的，填补了该方向关于非分支奇异集正则性的空白。

Abstract: We establish a sharp bound on the Hausdorff dimension of the non-branch singular set of branched stable minimal immersed hypersurfaces whose singular sets have locally finite $\mathcal H^{n-2}$-measure: the non-branch singular set is empty when $n=2$, discrete when $n=3$, and has Hausdorff dimension at most $n-3$ when $n\geq4$. We also construct a non-flat stable minimal cone in $\mathbb R^4$ arising from a branched minimal immersion whose vertex is a non-branch singularity. Taking products with Euclidean factors yields examples whose non-branch singular sets have Hausdorff dimension exactly $n-3$, showing that our regularity bound is sharp in every dimension $n\geq3$. The main ingredients in our proof are a generalized Schoen inequality and a corresponding branched sheeting theorem near stationary classical cones and unions of hyperplanes.

</details>


### [113] [The large mass limit of monopoles: abelian limits and Dirac singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29667)
*Daniel Fadel*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究渐近锥形3-流形上有限能量SU(2)磁单极子在大质量极限下的凝聚行为。当质量趋于无穷时，质量重正化能量测度集中在有限多个点处，其余区域上场发生指数级阿贝尔化。极限态在余集中收敛到带Dirac奇点的可约单极子，其奇异部分由凝聚点的加权0-环完全确定，逃逸电荷精确等于k减去各凝聚点总电荷之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大质量（或等价地，大尺度）磁单极子序列的极限行为，理解质量趋于无穷时能量如何凝聚、如何逃逸到无穷远，以及极限构型的结构——这是理解SU(2)磁单极子模空间紧致化与退化机制的核心问题。

Method: 利用质量重正化能量测度分析凝聚点与权重；通过沿单位Higgs方向的平移和规范变换构造收敛序列；证明在凝聚点补集上的指数级阿贝尔化；借助最小正Green函数与Dirac奇点理论刻画极限可约单极子。

Result: 证明了质量无穷大极限下能量集中于有限个点，权重为4π乘以各点处簇的总电荷；补集上场指数阿贝尔化并局部光滑收敛到由Green函数和Dirac奇点决定的可约单极子；奇异部分仅依赖于凝聚点与总电荷的加权0-环，不保留个体Euclidean轮廓；逃逸到无穷远的电荷精确为k减去所有凝聚点电荷之和。

Conclusion: 磁单极子大质量极限的凝聚结构由加权0-环完全刻画，能量逃逸量具有精确公式，极限的奇异部分粗粒化，不依赖内部层次结构。这为理解SU(2)磁单极子大质量退化极限提供了完整描述。

Abstract: Let $(A_i,Φ_i)$ be finite energy $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ monopoles of charge $k>0$ on an asymptotically conical $3$-manifold with one end, with masses $m_i\to\infty$. After passing to a subsequence, the mass-renormalized energy measures concentrate at finitely many points $x_a$ with concentration weights $4πK_a$, where $K_a$ is the total charge of the complete finite cluster of mass-one Euclidean monopoles lying over $x_a$. We prove that, on the complement $M$ of these points, the fields abelianize exponentially. After translating the Higgs fields by their masses along the unit Higgs directions and applying gauge transformations, the translated pairs converge smoothly locally to a reducible monopole $(A_\infty,Φ_\infty)$ of the form \[
  Φ_\infty=-uΨ_\infty,
  \qquad
  F_{A_\infty}=-*du\,Ψ_\infty,
  \qquad
  u=4π\sum_aK_aG(\,\cdot\,,x_a), \] where $Ψ_\infty$ is a parallel unit section and $G$ is the minimal positive Green function. Consequently, $x_a$ is a Dirac singularity of charge $K_a$. The singular part of the residual limit is determined by the weighted $0$-cycle of concentration points and total cluster charges, and does not retain the individual Euclidean profiles or their separation hierarchy. We also show that $k-\sum_aK_a$ is exactly the charge escaping through the asymptotically conical end, and describe the residual flat abelian ambiguity.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

### [114] [Entropy Geometry and Normalized Means on Infinite-Dimensional Hamiltonian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28660)
*Jean-Pierre Magnot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于无穷维哈密顿系统平衡态统计力学的几何-解析框架。在没有合适的σ-加性不变测度的情况下，利用规范化均值（normalized means）推广概率测度与规范化积分，在弱辛Fréchet流形上构造熵与自由能泛函，并在显式可容许性与分离假设下给出指数族平衡态的存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷维哈密顿系统（如流体力学和场论中的系统）通常缺乏合适的σ-加性不变测度，这使得经典统计力学框架难以直接应用。因此需要一种更广义的框架来处理这类系统的平衡态统计描述。

Method: 采用规范化均值替代传统概率测度，在弱辛Fréchet流形上构建熵和自由能泛函；通过可容许性与分离假设建立指数族平衡态的存在唯一性定理；利用Poisson-KMS恒等式刻画哈密顿流下的平稳性；借助Legendre-Fenchel对偶建立大尺度变量域上的凹熵。

Result: 指数族平衡态在保持参考均值和平衡权重不变的哈密顿流下是平稳的；当参考均值为Poisson不变时满足经典Poisson-KMS恒等式；在局部指数正则性假设下，对数配分泛函光滑且凸，其Hessian由协方差形式给出；在热力学零方向上严格凸模，并通过Legendre-Fenchel对偶诱导出凹熵。框架在Map(M,G)与Diff(M)上的H^s-测地线方程（含流体力学与场论例子）中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为缺乏不变测度的无穷维哈密顿系统提供了一套完整的平衡态统计力学几何-解析框架，并通过当前群与微分同胚群的实例展示了其广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We propose a geometric--analytic framework for equilibrium statistical mechanics on infinite-dimensional Hamiltonian systems. In situations where no suitable $σ$-additive invariant measure is available, we use \emph{normalized means}, which generalize probability measures and normalized integrals. This construction yields entropy and free-energy functionals on weak symplectic Fréchet manifolds and gives existence and uniqueness of exponential-family equilibrium states under explicit admissibility and separation assumptions. These states are stationary under Hamiltonian flows preserving both the reference mean and the equilibrium weight, and satisfy a classical Poisson--KMS identity when the reference mean is Poisson invariant. Under a local exponential regularity assumption, the logarithmic partition functional is smooth and convex, with Hessian given by the covariance form. It is strictly convex modulo thermodynamically null directions and, through Legendre--Fenchel duality, induces a concave entropy on the domain of extensive variables. We illustrate the framework with $H^s$-geodesic equations on current groups $\operatorname{Map}(M,G)$ and diffeomorphism groups $\operatorname{Diff}(M)$, including hydrodynamic and field-theoretic examples.

</details>


### [115] [Free to Interacting Map for Crystalline SPT Phases: Equivariance vs Crystalline Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28811)
*Daniel Sheinbaum,Omar Antolín-Camarena*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全等变的Freed-Hopkins方案推广，用于分类交互式SPT相，并将其应用于对称晶体的弱自由费米子，论证了等变FTI映射的存在以及完全等变性相比CEP的物理与数学优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Freed和Hopkins的ansatz被用于将自由费米子SPT相推广到交互SPT相，并已推广至晶体相与晶体等价原理(CEP)。然而，CEP在弱自由费米子情形下失效，且FTI映射也存在问题，因此需要寻找更自然的推广框架。

Method: 将Freed-Hopkins原始ansatz推广为对对称晶格对称群完全等变的版本，构造从对称晶格弱自由费米子到交互SPT相的等变FTI映射，并从数学与物理角度论证该方案比空间对称性扩展更自然。

Result: 证明了对称晶格弱自由费米子存在自然的等变FTI映射，并论证了完全等变ansatz在数学和物理上均比Freed-Hopkins的空间对称性扩展更自然，完全等变性应当取代CEP成立。

Conclusion: 等变推广的Freed-Hopkins ansatz为交互晶体SPT相的分类提供了更自然且正确的框架，弥补了CEP对弱自由费米子失效的问题。

Abstract: Freed and Hopkins developed an ansatz for classifying interacting SPT phases using invertible field theories with a natural Free to Interacting (FTI) map from free fermion phases. This ansatz has been generalized to include crystalline phases and a crystalline equivalence principle (CEP). However, motivated by failure of the CEP for weak free fermions and the FTI, here we generalize the original Freed and Hopkins ansatz to a fully equivariant version for symmorphic crystallographic symmetries and show there is a natural equivariant FTI map from symmorphic crystalline weak free fermions. We further discuss why this equivariant ansatz is both mathematically and physically more natural than the spatial symmetry extension by Freed and Hopkins and why full equivariance should hold over the CEP.

</details>


### [116] [Topology in One Point Interaction Problem on Extended Non-Local Star Graphs and its Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29146)
*Lung-Hui Chen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了星形度量图上Sturm-Liouville算子的逆谱问题，在包含非局部边界条件和非局部特征函数的情况下，通过构造顶点处连续的特殊解来恢复网络拓扑结构，并在给定拓扑条件下证明了系统的可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究星形度量图上带有非局部Sturm-Liouville算子的逆谱问题，将顶点作为控制/调节中心来建模随时间变化的振动或通量，旨在恢复网络的拓扑结构。

Method: 在星形图的顶点处施加单点相互作用条件，构造在一端固定且在顶点连续的特殊解，利用非局部特征函数来寻找非平凡的非局部特征值，从而进行逆谱分析。

Result: 证明了在给定拓扑条件下系统是可解的，并成功恢复了网络的拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 系统的可解性依赖于必要的条件，且该方法能够建模在顶点处按特定频率垂直振动的弦，为度量图上的逆谱问题提供了一种可行的分析框架。

Abstract: The author studies the inverse spectral problem of Sturm-Liouville operator on a star-like metric graph. At the vertex of this star-like graph, there are attached $m$ edges that imposed with non-local Sturm-Liouville operator satisfying some suitable non-local boundary conditions. At the vertex, we consider one point interaction condition to model a metric graph that fixed on the end of edges of the graph. This models the vibration or flux that changes over time that monitored at the vertex which serves as certain control/regulation center. The author shows that the system is solvable under very necessary conditions. It is crucial to recover the topology of the network/metric graph which the topology is given. To begin the analysis, one constructs the special solution fixed on one end of edges while maintaining continuous at the vertex. This models a string that is vibrating vertically at the vertex according to certain frequencies. The non-local characteristic function plays a role, and then, one tries to find a non-trivial non-local eigenvalue.

</details>


### [117] [Realization embeddings of the rank two Racah algebra into the rank two Jacobi algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29358)
*Nicolas Crampé,Sarah Post,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了秩二Racah代数R_2到秩二Jacobi代数J_2的显式嵌入。在三角形上的J_2微分模型中，通过三对角化将两个乘法算符提升为Racah型生成元，使五边形的每条边都成为秩一Racah代数，从而将R_2实现于J_2内部。文中还显式给出了不同可交换对子的共同本征函数（Gauss超几何函数与Jacobi多项式的乘积），并计算了涉及单变量Wilson多项式及Tratnik型双变量Racah多项式的重叠系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解秩二Racah代数R_2与秩二Jacobi代数J_2之间的结构关系是超特殊函数与代数表示论交叉领域的重要问题。Racah代数与Jacobi代数是量子可积系统和特殊函数理论中出现的两种关键代数结构，建立它们之间的显式嵌入关系有助于统一理解其表示论与正交多项式理论。

Method: 本文采用构造性方法：首先在三角形的微分模型中研究J_2的子代数结构（由五边形组织，上边为秩一Jacobi代数，底边为秩一Racah代数）；然后通过三对角化将两个乘法算符转化为Racah型生成元，使五边形的每条边均成为秩一Racah代数；进而显式构造R_2嵌入J_2的映射。共同本征函数通过Gauss超几何函数与Jacobi多项式的乘积给出，重叠系数通过Wilson多项式和Tratnik型双变量Racah多项式计算。

Result: 成功构造了R_2到J_2的显式嵌入；确定了不同可交换生成元对的共同本征函数及其显式表达式；计算了各对基底间的重叠系数，其中一对涉及Wilson多项式，另一对涉及Tratnik型双变量Racah多项式；同时回顾了J_2作为R_2的收缩（contraction）的已知结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了秩二Racah代数与秩二Jacobi代数之间的桥梁，通过嵌入关系统一了两者的表示论框架，并揭示了从Racah代数收缩到Jacobi代数以及从Jacobi代数嵌入Racah代数这一双向结构的丰富联系，为相关正交多项式理论提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We construct an explicit embedding of the rank two Racah algebra $\mathfrak{R}_2$ into the rank two Jacobi algebra $\mathfrak{J}_2$. Working in the differential model of $\mathfrak{J}_2$ on the triangle, we recall that the subalgebra structure of $\mathfrak{J}_2$ is organized by a pentagon whose four upper edges carry rank one Jacobi algebras and whose base carries a rank one Racah algebra. Promoting the two multiplication operators to Racah type generators by tridiagonalization turns every edge of the pentagon into a rank one Racah algebra and realizes $\mathfrak{R}_2$ inside $\mathfrak{J}_2$. We determine the common eigenfunctions of different commuting pairs explicitly as products of Gauss hypergeometric functions and Jacobi polynomials. Different overlap coefficients are computed involving univariate Wilson polynomials as well as, for one pair of bases, bivariate Racah polynomials of Tratnik type. We also recall how $\mathfrak{J}_2$ arises as a contraction of the rank two Racah algebra $\mathfrak{R}_2$.

</details>


### [118] [Haantjes torsion and integrability: a proof of Bolsinov-Konyaev-Matveev's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29373)
*Alessandro Arsie,Paolo Lorenzoni*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个由Bolsinov、Konyaev和Matveev提出的猜想：对于具有gl-正则算子A的水动力型系统在点p处的可积性，意味着A及其所有对称在p邻域内的Haantjes张量消失。基于此结果，在代数一般点邻域内，任何由gl-正则算子场定义的可积水动力型系统都可以表示为ut=X(u)∘ux的形式，其中X是向量场，∘是满足Hertling-Manin条件的交换结合乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机是验证关于水动力型系统可积性与Haantjes张量之间关系的猜想。Haantjes张量的消失是此类系统可积性的重要几何条件，但此前仅知道充分性（可积性蕴含Haantjes张量消失）在某些较弱意义下成立，本文旨在建立严格的双向联系，并利用此结果获得可积水动力型系统的规范形式。

Method: 论文采用几何与代数方法相结合的证明路线：首先证明在给定点p处gl-正则算子A的可积性蕴含A的Haantjes张量在p邻域内消失，进而推广到所有对称的Haantjes张量；然后借助文献[8]的结果，将这一结论应用于代数一般点邻域内的可积系统，将其规范化为由向量场X与满足Hertling-Manin条件的交换结合乘积∘描述的结构形式。

Result: 成功证明了Bolsinov-Konyaev-Matveev猜想，确立了可积水动力型系统与Haantjes张量消失之间的严格等价关系（至少在一个方向上），并推导出任何具有gl-正则算子的可积水动力型系统在代数一般点邻域内均可表示为ut=X(u)∘ux，其中∘为满足Hertling-Manin条件的Frobenius型乘法结构。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个重要的结构定理：可积水动力型系统在代数一般点邻域内具有特定的代数结构——由满足Hertling-Manin条件的交换结合乘积和相应的向量场所定义。这一结果不仅验证了既有猜想，还为可积水动力型系统的分类和进一步研究提供了强有力的规范形式工具。

Abstract: We prove a conjecture formulated by Bolsinov, Konyaev and Matveev in [7] stating that, integrability of a system of hydrodynamic type ${\bf u}_t=A({\bf u}) {\bf u}_x$ with $\mathfrak{gl}$-regular $A$ at a point $p$ implies the vanishing of the Haantjes tensor of $A$ and of all its symmetries in a neighborhood of $p$. As a consequence, leveraging on the result of [8], in a neighbourhood of an algebraically generic point, any integrable system of hydrodynamic type defined by a $\mathfrak{gl}$-regular operator field can be written as ${\bf u}_t=X({\bf u})\circ {\bf u}_x$ where $X$ is a vector field and $\circ$ is a commutative associative product satisfying Hertling-Manin conditions.

</details>


### [119] [On the Emergence of Exponential Decay from Discrete Spectra for Friedrichs Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29485)
*Tamim Al-Qaiwani,Sören Petrat*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类Friedrichs哈密顿量（基态与浴通过秩一扰动耦合），在浴哈密顿量具有纯离散谱的情况下，考虑谱测度弱收敛到具有Hölder连续密度的绝对连续极限测度时，激发态的存活概率在合适时间尺度上近似指数衰减，衰减率由Fermi黄金规则给出，误差通过耦合强度和Lévy距离估计。应用包括旋转波近似下大腔中两能级原子与无质量玻色子场的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解激发态在离散谱浴环境中的衰变动力学，特别是在谱测度弱收敛到连续极限时，如何从Fermi黄金规则出发获得近似的指数衰减行为，并给出误差的定量估计。

Method: 考虑Friedrichs哈密顿量序列，其谱测度弱收敛到具有Hölder连续密度的绝对连续极限测度。利用Lévy距离量化离散谱测度与极限测度之间的偏差，在此基础上证明存活概率在合适时间尺度上的近似指数衰减，衰减率由极限测度的Fermi黄金规则给出。

Result: 证明了在给定条件下，激发态存活概率按指数方式近似衰减，衰减率由极限测度对应的Fermi黄金规则确定；误差以耦合强度和Lévy距离为参数进行估计。将该结果应用于旋转波近似下大腔中的两能级原子系统。

Conclusion: 离散谱浴系统在大腔极限（谱测度收敛到连续极限）下的衰变行为可用Fermi黄金规则近似描述，误差受到耦合强度和谱测度收敛速度的控制。

Abstract: We study a class of Friedrichs Hamiltonians, that is, operators describing an excited state coupled to a bath through a rank-one perturbation, in the case where the bath Hamiltonian has purely discrete spectrum. We consider sequences of such Hamiltonians for which the spectral measures associated to the coupling functions by the bath Hamiltonians converge weakly to a limiting measure that is absolutely continuous with a Hölder continuous density near the excited energy. Under this assumption, we show that the survival probability of the excited state decays in an approximately exponential manner on suitable time scales and under favourable conditions, with a decay rate given by Fermi's golden rule for the limiting measure. The error is estimated in terms of the coupling strength and the Lévy distance between the discrete spectral measures and the limiting measure. As an application, we treat a two-level atom coupled to a massless bosonic field confined to a large cavity in the rotating-wave approximation.

</details>


### [120] [Spectral Analysis of a Soft Quantum Waveguide Coupled to a Massive Quantum Scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29526)
*Benjamin Alvarez,Hugo Gouttenegre*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个Nelson型哈密顿量，描述在平面软量子波导中运动的无自旋非相对论性粒子与大规模标量玻色子场的线性耦合。通过将弯曲波导与直波导比较，证明了直哈密顿量的谱是纯本质且无间隙的，并在弯曲情形下建立了本质谱下界的HVZ型公式及离散谱存在的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软量子波导中粒子与玻色子场耦合的谱性质，特别关注弯曲几何对谱结构的影响，为理解曲率诱导的束缚态现象提供数学基础。

Method: 通过纤维分解研究直波导哈密顿量（利用纵向平移不变性）；将弯曲波导与直波导进行对比，建立HVZ型公式刻画本质谱下界；利用有效一维Schrödinger算子推导离散谱非空的充分条件。

Result: 直哈密顿量的谱为纯本质且无间隙；弯曲哈密顿量的本质谱下界由直系统的谱和单玻色子阈值共同决定（HVZ型公式）；得到了弯曲情形下离散谱存在的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了弯曲软量子波导与玻色子场耦合系统的完整谱分析框架，揭示了弯曲几何可诱导离散谱（束缚态）出现，并给出了量化判据。

Abstract: We study a Nelson-type Hamiltonian describing a massive spinless non-relativistic particle moving in a planar soft quantum waveguide and linearly coupled to a massive scalar bosonic field. Our spectral analysis relies on comparing the curved waveguide with its straight counterpart. For the straight Hamiltonian, longitudinal translation invariance yields a fiber decomposition, and we prove that its spectrum is purely essential and gapless. For the curved Hamiltonian, we establish an HVZ-type formula for the bottom of the essential spectrum, involving that of the straight system and the one-boson threshold. We also derive a sufficient condition for the existence of non-empty discrete spectrum in the curved case, expressed in terms of an effective one-dimensional Schrödinger operator.

</details>
